Component-level fusion is then performed between semantically aligned representations for structure preservation and rain suppression. We further introduce event-domain supervision to regularize sparse event reconstruction, structural consistency, and gradient orientation. Experiments on synthetic and real RGB-Event video deraining datasets demonstrate superior quantitative performance, visual quality, and downstream perception robustness. Code is available at https://github.com/AE86-plus/RainDancer.

</details>


### [66] [AspectCLIP: Optimizing CLIP Representation Space via Aspect-Guided Consistency Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13805)
*Yiyang Yao,Shanglin Liu,Jianming Lv,Chengjun Wang,Jinyi Li,Yuchan Jie,Zhihua Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AspectCLIP reformulates consistency regularization in CLIP to address the information asymmetry between images and text, where captions describe only one aspect of an image. It partitions samples into attribute clusters based on textual similarity, applies full cyclic consistency within clusters and prototype-level comparisons across clusters, enforcing alignment only when describing consistent facets. Outperforms traditional methods on downstream tasks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing contrastive learning methods enforce global consistency regularization, ignoring the inherent information asymmetry between images and text: captions describe only one specific aspect of an image, so visually similar images can be paired with completely divergent textual content. This introduces semantic distortion in the representation space by imposing constraints between visually similar images whose captions describe divergent aspects.

Method: AspectCLIP partitions training samples into attribute clusters based on textual similarity to identify aspect-coherent groups. It then applies full cyclic consistency within each cluster while restricting cross-cluster regularization to prototype-level comparisons, enforcing strict geometric alignment only when images and texts describe a consistent facet.

Result: Extensive experiments on downstream tasks demonstrate that AspectCLIP consistently outperforms traditional methods and achieves a more structured representation space.

Conclusion: AspectCLIP effectively respects the one-to-many structure of image-text pairs, reducing semantic distortion and improving representation learning by applying aspect-guided regularization.

Abstract: Contrastive Language-Image Pretraining learns a shared representation space through large-scale contrastive learning. However, existing methods that enforce global consistency regularization overlook a key challenge: the inherent information asymmetry between images and text: captions typically describe only one specific aspect of an image, thus images with similar visual content can be paired with completely divergent textual content and semantic information. Consequently, global regularizers inadvertently impose constraints between visually similar images whose captions describe divergent aspects, introducing semantic distortion into the representation space. We propose AspectCLIP, a framework that reformulates consistency regularization to respect this one-to-many structure. AspectCLIP first partitions training samples into attribute clusters based on textual similarity to identify aspect-coherent groups, then applies full cyclic consistency within each cluster while restricting cross-cluster regularization to prototype-level comparisons. This aspect-guided regularization enforces strict geometric alignment only when images and texts describe a consistent facet, while allowing flexibility across divergent aspects. Extensive experiments on downstream tasks demonstrate that AspectCLIP consistently outperforms traditional methods and achieves a more structured representation space.

</details>


### [67] [Bake It Till You Make It: Ultrafast Spatial Texture-Atlas Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13808)
*Neel Kelkar,Simon Niedermayr,Kaloian Petkov,Klaus Engel,Rüdiger Westermann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种解耦辐射表示方法，将低频几何/视点相关外观用2D surfels建模，高频纹理用视点无关的空间哈希网格烘培到紧凑纹理图集，结合稀疏性增强优化（惩罚半透明和逐原素衰减），实现比3DGS高达5倍的速度提升，支持消费级硬件上实时4K 60FPS渲染，同时保持最先进的视觉保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS扩展使用哈希网格外观参数化能捕捉精细颜色细节，但片段渲染计算成本高昂。需要在不牺牲质量的前提下显著加速渲染。

Method: 解耦辐射表示：低频几何和视点相关外观用2D surfels表示；高频纹理通过视点无关的空间哈希网格编码并烘培到紧凑纹理图集。引入稀疏性增强优化（惩罚半透明和逐原素衰减），激进地修剪不重要的surfels，实现更稀疏的重建。

Result: 相比3DGS实现高达5倍加速，重建更快更稀疏，视觉保真度保持最先进水平，在消费级GPU上实现实时4K 60FPS渲染。

Conclusion: 该方法在保持高质量的同时大幅提升了3DGS的渲染效率，适合实时高分辨率应用，通过几何稀疏性和高效GPU纹理映射实现性能突破。

Abstract: Recent extensions of 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) capture fine color details using hash-grid-based appearance parameterization but incur high computational cost during fragment rendering. We introduce a decoupled radiance representation that models low-frequency geometry and view dependent appearance features with 2D surfels while representing high-frequency textures via a view-independent spatial hash grid that is baked into a compact texture atlas. By including sparsity-enhancing optimizations that penalize semi-transparency and per-primitive falloff, our method aggressively prunes insignificant surfels and achieves significantly faster and sparser reconstructions than prior work. Exploiting geometric sparsity and efficient GPU texture mapping, our approach achieves up to a fivefold speedup over 3DGS while preserving state-of-the-art visual fidelity, enabling real-time 4K rendering at 60 FPS on consumer hardware.

</details>


### [68] [Multimodal Assessment of Pancreatic Cancer Resectability Using Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13826)
*Vincent Ochs,Christoph Kuemmerli,Florentin Bieder,Julia Wolleb,Joel L. Lavanchy,Julia Ruppel,Jan Liechti,Stephanie Taha-Mehlitz,Christian Andreas Nebiker,Beat Mueller,Giuseppe Kito Fusai,Joerg-Matthias Pollok,Anas Taha,Philippe C. Cattin,Sebastian Staubli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种全自动多模态深度学习框架，联合分析3D增强CT和结构化临床信息，依据NCCN指南将胰腺导管腺癌患者分为可切除、交界可切除和局部晚期三类。该方法使用Swin-UNETR骨干网络辅助分割胰腺、肿瘤和血管以获取解剖感知特征，并与17个临床变量的嵌入融合，通过动态多任务训练优化分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胰腺导管腺癌（PDAC）可切除性的准确判断依赖于CT图像中肿瘤与主要胰周血管的关系评估，但专家评估存在显著变异。需要一种自动化、标准化的方法以减少主观差异，提高分类准确性。

Method: 采用Swin-UNETR作为骨干网络，对3D增强CT进行胰腺、肿瘤和血管的辅助分割，提取解剖感知的图像特征；同时从17个常规临床变量中生成紧凑的临床嵌入；将图像特征与临床嵌入融合后输入轻量级分类头进行NCCN三分类。训练过程使用动态多任务目标，根据当前肿瘤Dice性能自适应调整分割与分类任务的权重，以促进既具有解剖信息又具有判别能力的特征表示。

Result: 实验表明，该多模态框架能有效融合影像与临床信息，通过动态多任务学习提升特征表示质量，在PDAC可切除性分类上取得较好性能（具体数值需参考全文）。

Conclusion: 所提出的全自动多模态深度学习框架能够利用CT影像和临床数据，通过解剖感知的联合分割与分类学习，实现PDAC的NCCN可切除性准确分类，有望为临床决策提供标准化辅助工具。

Abstract: Accurate determination of pancreatic ductal adenocarcinoma (PDAC) resectability relies on evaluating how the tumor interacts with major peripancreatic vessels on CT imaging, yet expert assessment often shows substantial variability. We introduce a fully automated multimodal deep learning framework that jointly analyzes 3D contrast enhanced CT and structured clinical information to classify patients into the three National Comprehensive Cancer Network (NCCN) resectability categories (upfront resectable, borderline resectable, locally advanced). The approach uses a Swin-UNETR backbone to obtain anatomy aware image representations through auxiliary segmentation of pancreas, tumor, and vascular structures. These features are fused with a compact clinical embedding derived from 17 routinely collected variables and processed by a lightweight classification head. Model training is guided by a dynamic multitask objective that adapts the balance between segmentation and classification based on current tumor Dice performance, promoting feature representations that remain both anatomically informed and discriminative.

</details>


### [69] [Towards Enhancing 3D Spatial Reasoning in Medical Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13860)
*Zhuoyuan Fu,Zeshang Li,Yiqiong Zhang,Hangui Lin,Yan Shu,Yan Li,Binyang Li,Yaru Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对3D医学影像理解中标注成本高、数据集不透明以及缺乏显式临床推理的问题，提出了一种基于切片级数据合成范式的大规模结构化推理数据集。该方法模拟放射科医生的诊断流程，将复杂的3D阅读过程分解为逐切片观察并合成为可解释的思维链，强制遵循临床原则（空间顺序追踪、多切片空间感知、鉴别排除）。通过指令微调2D预训练MLLM，在多个3D医学基准上显著提升性能，展现了强大的空间推理能力，媲美原生3D架构，缩小了性能差距。这种数据驱动的策略无需昂贵的3D预训练即可实现深度体积理解和高可解释性临床逻辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在2D医学图像理解上成功，但扩展到3D体积成像受限于高昂的标注成本和数据集的不透明性。当前数据格式（如僵硬的VQA对或非结构化的最终临床报告）通常无法捕捉显式的临床推理过程，限制了模型的可解释性和诊断能力。

Method: 提出一种新颖的切片级数据合成范式，模拟放射科医生真实诊断流程：将复杂的3D读取过程分解为细粒度的逐切片观察，然后将这些观察合成为可解释的思维链（Chain-of-Thought, CoT）。该合成推理框架强制贯彻关键临床原则：顺序空间追踪、多切片空间感知（用于伪影缓解）和鉴别排除。基于此构建大规模结构化推理数据集，用于指令微调标准的2D预训练MLLM基线，以增强其体积理解能力。

Result: 在多个3D医学基准上的综合评估显示，该方法相比2D基线有显著性能提升。所得模型展现出鲁棒的空间推理能力，并媲美资源密集型的原生3D架构，有效弥合了性能差距。

Conclusion: 这种以数据为中心的策略无需计算昂贵的3D特定预训练，即可实现深度体积理解和高度可解释的临床逻辑。完整仓库（包括数据集和训练流程）已公开在https://github.com/2020420145009/hounsfield。

Abstract: While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated remarkable success in 2D medical image understanding, their extension to 3D volumetric imaging remains hindered by prohibitive annotation costs and dataset opacity. Current data formats, predominantly consisting of rigid Visual Question Answering (VQA) pairs or unstructured final clinical reports, typically fail to capture explicit clinical reasoning. To address this limitation, we introduce a large-scale structured reasoning dataset constructed via a novel slice-wise data synthesis paradigm. Inspired by the genuine diagnostic workflow of radiologists, this paradigm models visual cognition by decomposing the complex 3D reading process, translating global clinical priors into fine-grained, per-slice observations that are subsequently synthesized into an interpretable Chain-of-Thought (CoT). Crucially, this synthesized reasoning framework enforces essential clinical principles: sequential spatial tracking, multi-slice spatial awareness for artifact mitigation, and differential exclusion. To validate this approach, we instruction-tune a standard 2D-pretrained MLLM baseline using the synthesized data to enhance its volumetric comprehension. Comprehensive evaluations across multiple 3D medical benchmarks demonstrate that our method yields significant performance improvements over the 2D baseline. Furthermore, the resulting model exhibits robust spatial reasoning capabilities and rivals resource-intensive native 3D architectures, effectively bridging the performance gap. Ultimately, this data-centric strategy unlocks deep volumetric understanding and highly interpretable clinical logic without requiring computationally expensive 3D-specific pre-training. The complete repository, including datasets and training workflows, is publicly available at https://github.com/2020420145009/hounsfield.

</details>


### [70] [Unleashing Multimodal Large Language Models for Training-free HOI Detection in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13881)
*Ting Lei,Jialin Liu,Zhu Xu,Yuxin Peng,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AgentHOI，一个无需训练的代理框架，通过协调基础视觉模块的多模态推理能力实现开放世界的人-物交互检测，引入上下文感知多轮推理和多面交互定位机制，在真实场景中超越现有监督和弱监督方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的HOI检测方法依赖于预定义交互类别的监督学习，限制了其在开放世界和组合场景的泛化能力。现有的基于MLLM的提示策略主要提取判别性表示，未能充分利用基础模型固有的多模态推理能力，因此难以处理模糊和开放世界交互场景中的上下文推理。

Method: 提出AgentHOI框架：1）无需训练，将基础模型的通用多模态推理能力模块化地迁移到HOI检测；2）引入上下文感知多轮推理机制，逐步细化交互假设，确保穷尽性和组合性的HOI发现；3）引入多面交互定位机制，通过生成整合语义、空间和外观线索的实例特定描述来增强定位精度。

Result: 大量实验表明，AgentHOI在真实场景中性能优于现有的监督和弱监督方法，且无需任何HOI数据进行训练。

Conclusion: AgentHOI证明了通过代理化协调基础模型的多模态能力，可以在不依赖特定任务训练数据的情况下，有效解决开放世界的HOI检测问题，为摆脱数据集特定监督的泛化性HOI检测提供了新范式。

Abstract: Human-object interaction detection (HOID) has traditionally been formulated as a supervised detection problem over predefined interaction categories. While such paradigms achieve strong performance on closed-set benchmarks, they fundamentally entangle interaction understanding with dataset-specific supervision, limiting their ability to generalize to open-world and compositional scenarios. Recent HOI detectors attempt to leverage MLLMs through prompting strategies to transfer interaction-specific knowledge. However, such prompt-based approaches primarily focus on extracting discriminative representations from pretrained models, while underexploring their inherent multimodal reasoning capabilities. As a result, they struggle to provide informative contextual reasoning for ambiguous and open-world interaction scenarios. In this work, we present AgentHOI, a training-free, agentic framework that transfers the generalist multimodal reasoning capabilities of foundation models to HOI detection in the wild. Instead of learning interaction classifiers, AgentHOI modularly orchestrates complementary vision foundation modules to perform open-ended semantic reasoning and spatial grounding in a coordinated manner. To address the challenges of incomplete interaction discovery and ambiguous localization in complex scenes, we introduce two key mechanisms: (1) Context-aware Multi-round Reasoning, which progressively refines interaction hypotheses to ensure exhaustive and compositional HOI discovery, and (2) Multifaceted Interaction Localization, which enhances grounding precision by generating instance-specific descriptions that integrate semantic, spatial, and appearance cues. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AgentHOI achieves superior performance over state-of-the-art supervised and weakly supervised methods in real-world settings, despite requiring no HOID data for training.

</details>


### [71] [Fine-Grained Vision-Language Pretraining with Organ-Conditioned Pattern Tokens for CT Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13892)
*Guoliang You,Xiaomeng Chu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 OCP-CT，一种基于器官条件化模式-标记对齐的 CT 视觉-语言预训练框架。通过引入稀疏混合专家路由、可学习槽位和结构化软目标，实现比全局对比和粗粒度器官对齐更精细的放射学模式级对齐，在零样本异常诊断上取得 SOTA 结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 CT 视觉-语言预训练方法要么采用全局扫描-报告对比，虽可扩展但模糊了异质性器官证据；要么进行直接的器官级对齐，但同一器官可呈现多种放射学表现，对齐仍然粗粒度。因此需要更精细的对齐单元：器官条件化的放射学模式。

Method: 提出 OCP-CT 框架，保留稳定的全局 CT-报告对比分支，并引入器官条件模式接口：利用稀疏混合专家 (MoE) 将图像和文本标记根据潜在放射学模式进行路由，可学习槽位将路由后的标记查询为连续模式标记，然后通过基于报告推导的临床相似性构建的软目标进行图像-文本模式标记对比对齐。

Result: 在 CT-RATE 和 RAD-ChestCT 基准上，零样本异常诊断的平均 AUROC 分别达到 84.5% 和 69.9%，相比先前最强结果绝对提升 6.7 和 0.8 个百分点。

Conclusion: OCP-CT 通过器官条件化的放射学模式对齐，显著提升了 CT 视觉-语言预训练的性能，验证了更精细对齐单元的有效性。

Abstract: Computed tomography (CT) vision-language pretraining from paired volumes and radiology reports is a scalable yet challenging task. Existing methods commonly adopt global scan-report contrast, which is scalable but obscures heterogeneous organ evidence. Meanwhile, direct organ-level alignment remains coarse, since the same anatomy can exhibit multiple distinct radiological appearances. Therefore, pretraining requires a finer alignment unit: the organ-conditioned radiological pattern. In this work, we propose OCP-CT, an organ-conditioned pattern-token alignment framework for CT vision-language pretraining. Specifically, OCP-CT preserves a stable global CT-report contrastive branch and introduces an organ pattern interface: sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) routes image and text tokens according to latent radiological patterns, learnable slots query the routed tokens into continuous pattern tokens, and paired token contrast aligns image-text pattern tokens with structured soft targets built from report-derived clinical similarity. On the publicly available CT-RATE and RAD-ChestCT benchmarks, OCP-CT achieves average AUROCs of 84.5% and 69.9% for zero-shot abnormality diagnosis, respectively. Compared with the strongest prior reported results, these results yield absolute AUROC gains of 6.7 and 0.8 percentage points.

</details>


### [72] [The 2nd International StepUP Competition for Biometric Footstep Recognition: From Steps to Strides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13905)
*Robyn Larracy,Anant Gupta,Gourav Gupta,Ethan Eddy,Maxime Devanne,Cyril Meyer,Jin-Chern Chiou,Yueh-Shan Lee,Zong-Han Lu,Aaron Tabor,Erik Scheme*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 第二届国际StepUP步态识别竞赛利用StepUP-P150数据集（15万人、20万+高分辨率动态步足迹），聚焦三个挑战：未知用户泛化（少量注册数据）、跨域鲁棒性（鞋类和速度变化）、左右脚融合。最佳系统（ArogyaPandit团队）采用时空CNN+集成评分策略达到8.00%等错误率，表明时序模式和推理时归一化/校准策略的有效性，但识别未见过的个人鞋类仍具挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推动基于压力的步态生物特征识别研究，通过标准化且具有挑战性的评估框架，解决现有方法在未知用户泛化、跨域迁移（鞋类、速度变化）和多步融合等方面的不足，促进该领域向实际应用发展。

Method: 竞赛采用StepUP-P150数据集（150人、20万+高分辨率动态步足迹），设置三项挑战：1) 用少量注册样本识别未见用户；2) 对鞋类和行走速度变化的域偏移鲁棒性；3) 左右脚步态信息的有效融合。引入了更极端的跨域条件和步幅级验证（而非孤立步态），并采用未公开测试集进行标准化评估。

Result: 26个注册参赛者，最佳系统（ArogyaPandit研究团队）使用时空CNN结合集成评分策略达到8.00%等错误率。顶级方案展示了利用时序模式、推理时归一化和校准策略的价值，但识别未见过的个人鞋类仍具挑战，尤其存在特征相似的干扰项时。

Conclusion: 竞赛证明了压力步态识别在跨域场景下的潜力，时空建模和评分校准是提升性能的关键方向；但个人鞋类变化导致的域偏移仍是主要瓶颈，需进一步研究更鲁棒的特征表示和域适应技术。

Abstract: The International StepUP Competition Series was launched to advance research in pressure-based footstep biometrics through a standardized and challenging evaluation framework. Using the large-scale StepUP-P150 dataset (with more than 200,000 high-resolution dynamic footsteps from 150 individuals) and a previously unreleased test set, the 2nd edition of the competition addressed three key challenges: (1) generalization to unseen users with limited enrollment data, (2) robustness to domain shift caused by variations in footwear and walking speed and (3) effective fusion of paired left-right footsteps. While the first two challenges built on the inaugural competition, this edition introduced more extreme cross-domain conditions and moved beyond isolated footsteps to stride-level verification, enabling new opportunities for representation learning and inter-step information fusion. The competition attracted 26 registrants from academia and industry, with a best equal error rate of 8.00% achieved by the ArogyaPandit Research Team using a spatiotemporal CNN combined with an ensemble-based scoring strategy. The top solutions showcase the value of harnessing temporal patterns and of incorporating inference-time normalization and calibration strategies to improve scoring. However, the results also reveal that recognizing users in unseen personal footwear remains a challenge, especially in the presence of distractors with similar characteristics.

</details>


### [73] [Thresholded Cross-Attention for Reliable Intensity-Chromaticity Fusion in Low-Light Image Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13925)
*Yanyi Wu,Xu Zhang,Junkai Chen,Laibin Chang,Jiaqi Ma,Shi Chen,Linwei Zhu,Jianglei Di,Huan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 TCA-Net，通过阈值化交叉注意力（Thresholded Cross-Attention）在 HVI 空间中实现可靠的亮度-色度融合，解决固定 Top-K 稀疏注意力与层依赖置信度不匹配的问题，并辅以相位引导傅里叶交互模块、解耦双流引导模块和尺度感知一致性正则化，在多个低光照增强数据集上取得 competitive 结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 HVI 的方法通过解耦强度和色度来缓解颜色纠缠，但两个流融合的可靠性被忽视；固定配额 Top-K 稀疏注意力与层依赖的交叉注意力置信度不匹配，导致一些层丢弃有用依赖而另一些层保留噪声。

Method: TCA-Net 的核心是阈值化交叉注意力（TCA），用固定置信度阈值替代刚性 Top-K 配额，实现输入自适应和层自适应的保留基数，仅保留高置信度的跨流交互；配合相位引导傅里叶交互模块为强度流提供结构感知的亮度初始化，解耦双流引导模块构建残差强度特征抑制色度泄漏，以及尺度感知一致性正则化增强结构鲁棒性。

Result: 在 LOL-v1、LOL-v2、Sony-Total-Dark 和 LSRW-Huawei 数据集上，TCA-Net 实现了具有竞争力的恢复精度、更好的色彩保真度和紧凑的参数规模。

Conclusion: TCA-Net 通过自适应阈值化交叉注意力机制有效提升了 HVI 空间中强度-色度融合的可靠性，在低光照图像增强任务中兼顾了噪声抑制、色彩保真度和效率。

Abstract: Low-Light Image Enhancement (LLIE) requires a careful balance among noise suppression, color fidelity, and efficiency. Recent HVI-based methods alleviate color entanglement by decoupling intensity and chromaticity, yet how reliably the two streams are fused again is an overlooked factor that largely determines the final quality. We observe that the confidence of cross-stream attention is strongly layer-dependent, so the fixed-quota selection of Top-K sparse attention is mismatched to it, discarding informative dependencies in some layers while retaining noisy ones in others. Motivated by this observation, we propose TCA-Net, a network built around Thresholded Cross-Attention that targets reliable intensity-chromaticity fusion in the HVI space rather than introducing yet another color representation. At its core, TCA replaces the rigid Top-K quota with a fixed confidence threshold whose retained cardinality is input- and layer-adaptive, retaining only high-confidence cross-stream interactions while suppressing unreliable ones. Around this core, two complementary designs clean up the fusion before and after it: a Phase-guided Fourier Interaction Module provides a structure-aware brightness initialization for the intensity stream prior to fusion, and a Decoupled Dual-Stream Guidance Module constructs residual intensity features to suppress chromaticity leakage during reconstruction. A Scale-Aware Consistency Regularization further improves structural robustness under scale perturbations during training. Extensive experiments on LOL-v1, LOL-v2, Sony-Total-Dark, and LSRW-Huawei demonstrate that TCA-Net delivers competitive restoration accuracy, improved color fidelity, and a compact parameter size.

</details>


### [74] [Cyclone: Diffusion Model for Cycle-Consistent Weather Editing from Unpaired Driving Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13927)
*Thang-Anh-Quan Nguyen,Moussab Bennehar,Luis Guillermo Roldao Jimenez,Nathan Piasco,Dzmitry Tsishkou,Laurent Caraffa,Jean-Philippe Tarel,Roland Brémond*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Cyclone是一个基于潜在扩散的统一天气编辑框架，利用循环一致性约束和图像-文本模型知识，无需配对数据即可生成多种逼真天气效果，在多个自动驾驶感知任务中带来一致性改进，并可蒸馏为视频扩散模型实现时序一致的天气编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶系统在多样天气条件下的可靠感知仍是一大挑战。现有方法要么依赖合成数据增强，要么使用基于物理的任务特定模型，但这些方法需要配对训练数据，生成的天气效果不够真实，且对域外场景的泛化能力不足。

Method: 提出了Cyclone框架，基于潜在扩散模型，引入循环一致性约束，并利用图像-文本模型的知识，实现无需配对数据的多场景多天气条件编辑。

Result: 相比现有基线方法，Cyclone能生成更真实、保持结构完整性的输出，并在多个下游驾驶感知任务上带来一致性的性能提升。此外，该方法可蒸馏为视频扩散模型，实现时序一致的天气编辑。

Conclusion: Cyclone框架有效解决了天气编辑中配对数据依赖和生成真实感不足的问题，为自动驾驶感知系统在复杂天气下的鲁棒性提升提供了新的统一解决方案。

Abstract: Reliable perception under diverse weather conditions remains a major challenge for autonomous driving systems. A common strategy to improve robustness is either to synthesize adverse weather conditions for training perception models or to apply weather-removal techniques to recover clean inputs. However, existing approaches typically rely on synthetic data augmentation or physics-based, task-specific models that require paired training data and often struggle to generate realistic weather effects or generalize robustly to out-of-domain scenarios. Toward this problem, we present Cyclone, a unified framework for weather editing based on latent diffusion, equipped with cycle-consistent constraints and knowledge from image-text models. Cyclone enables the generation of multiple weather conditions across diverse scenes while eliminating the need for paired data. Experimental results show that our approach produces more realistic, structure-preserving outputs than existing baselines and leads to consistent improvements across several downstream driving perception tasks. Furthermore, we demonstrate that Cyclone can be distilled to a video diffusion model for temporally consistent weather editing.

</details>


### [75] [SIVA-RL: Sensitivity-Invariance Visual Alignment for Multimodal Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13931)
*Cheng Tang,Junzhi Ning,Min Cen,Wei Li,Xinyi Zeng,Pinxian Zeng,Rongbin Li,Qiming Zhu,Yuqiang Li,Junjun He,Yirong Chen,Ming Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SIVA-RL 框架，通过样本级、结果条件的视觉干预监督（而非固定干预类型）来增强多模态推理中视觉语言模型的视觉接地能力，在9个基准上提升3B/7B模型性能，最高提升14.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RLVR 方法仅关注答案正确性，无法保证模型预测基于视觉证据；而基于视觉干预的方法根据干预类型分配监督，忽略了同一种干预在不同样本上产生异质效果，导致监督信号不准确。

Method: 提出 SIVA-RL 框架：(1) 通过 token 对齐、距离约束的图内 PatchSwap 构造局部干预图像；(2) 使用冻结的审计策略对原始-干预图像对打分，以奖励下降幅度作为软路由权重；(3) 大下降对驱动敏感性对齐（鼓励模型关注变化区域），小下降对驱动不变性对齐（保持原答案），模糊对降权；(4) 兼容 GRPO 和 DAPO 训练骨干。

Result: 在 9 个多模态推理基准（涵盖数学、逻辑、视觉依赖任务）上，3B 和 7B 模型在所有设置下均优于对应的 RL 基线；视觉依赖推理任务提升 8.79 个百分点，4 种 GRPO/DAPO 配置下总体相对提升最高达 14.9%。

Conclusion: 将干预构造与监督分配解耦，基于实际奖励变化而非干预类型进行正则化，能更有效地提升视觉语言模型的视觉接地能力，且具有通用性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) drives multimodal reasoning, but answer-level correctness does not guarantee that a vision-language model grounds its predictions in visual evidence. Existing visual-intervention methods contrast policy behavior on original and modified images, yet assign supervision by the type of intervention rather than its observed effect. This assumption fails: identical operators produce heterogeneous outcomes across samples. We propose SIVA-RL, a Sensitivity-Invariance Visual Alignment framework that replaces operator-conditioned regularization with sample-wise, outcome-conditioned supervision. SIVA-RL constructs localized interventions through token-aligned, distance-constrained within-image PatchSwap. A frozen audit policy then scores each clean-intervention pair, and the observed reward drop becomes soft routing weights. Large-drop pairs drive sensitivity alignment, low-drop pairs drive clean-anchored invariance alignment, and ambiguous pairs are down-weighted. This design decouples intervention construction from supervision assignment and is compatible with both GRPO and DAPO backbones. Across nine multimodal reasoning benchmarks spanning mathematical, logical, and vision-dependent tasks, SIVA-RL improves 3B and 7B models over matched RL baselines in every setting. It yields an 8.79 percentage-point gain on vision-dependent reasoning and up to 14.9% relative overall improvement across all four GRPO- and DAPO-based configurations.

</details>


### [76] [A novel unsupervised machine learning strategy to handle multimodal cardiac PET/MRI data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13936)
*Brunnhilde Ponsi,Thomas Carlier,Lara Marteau,Aurélien Monnet,Thomas Eugène,Jean-Michel Serfaty,Nicolas Piriou,Hatem Necib*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种基于PET/MRI多参数定量分析的方法，通过两步聚类（患者内超体素聚类+患者间谱聚类）识别致心律失常性左室心肌病（ALVC）的异常区域，并自动生成文本和牛眼图报告。在99例ALVC患者和167个数值体模上验证，平衡准确率分别为0.76±0.04和≥0.8，表明该方法能系统处理多模态数据，辅助临床诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 致心律失常性左室心肌病（ALVC）缺乏金标准诊断标准，诊断困难。同时PET/MR成像结合多参数定量分析有助于识别与表型和进展相关的不同特征，但缺乏系统性的数据处理方法。

Method: 对99例基因确诊ALVC患者的T1、T2 mapping、LGE和18F-FDG-PET图像进行两步聚类：首先对每位患者图像进行z-score标准化并叠加，聚类成超体素；然后通过谱聚类得到32个患者间超体素组。为每个聚类和模态分配异常评分，可视化异常区域，并自动生成文本和牛眼图报告。在167个数值体模上进一步验证。

Result: 在99例患者上，重复嵌套交叉验证的平衡准确率为0.76±0.04；在167个数值体模上平衡准确率≥0.8。识别出的异常聚类与心脏医师的视觉观察高度吻合，有助于区分不同程度的纤维化或炎症。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了对ALVC患者PET/MRI多模态数据的系统化处理，能够表征心肌异质性，自动生成与临床评估一致的报告，具有辅助诊断的潜力。

Abstract: Arrhythmogenic left ventricular cardiomyopathy is a genetic myocardial disease difficult to diagnose due to the lack of gold standard criteria. Simultaneous PET/MR imaging, combined with multiparametric quantitative analysis, could facilitate the identification of different profiles related to the phenotype and progression of cardiomyopathy. This preliminary study focuses on a methodological strategy for dealing with PET/MRI data, including inter-patient data linkage and regional analysis. Two-step clustering was applied to T1 and T2 maps, LGE, and 18F-FDG-PET images of 99 patients genetically diagnosed with arrhythmogenic left ventricular cardiomyopathy. Each patient's images were independently z-scored and summed into a single volume, which was clustered into supervoxels. Thirty-two inter-patient groups of supervoxels were obtained by spectral clustering. An "abnormality" score was assigned to each cluster and modality, and used to visualise abnormal regions likely associated with disease. They enabled the generation of automated textual and bullseye health reports for each patient, which were compared with cardiac imager assessments using balanced accuracy in repeated nested cross-validation. This approach was further validated on a larger cohort of 167 numerical phantoms. The reports generated by clustering accurately identified most of the cardiac physicians' observations (BA = 0.76 $\pm$ 0.04 in repeated nested cross-validation on patients, and BA $\ge$ 0.8 on phantoms). Furthermore, the identified abnormal clusters closely matched their visual observations, facilitating the identification of varying degrees of fibrosis or inflammation on the images. This approach enables a more systematic handling of multimodal PET/MRI data to characterise myocardial heterogeneity in arrhythmogenic left ventricular cardiomyopathy patients.

</details>


### [77] [Peak-End-Net: A Peak-End Rule Inspired Framework for Generalizable Video Aesthetic Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13941)
*Geng Li,Haiwen Li,Rui Chen,Jing Tang,Lei Sun,Xiangxiang Chu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: The paper proposes Peak-End-Net, a lightweight and interpretable framework for video aesthetic assessment (VAA), inspired by the peak-end rule from psychology. It transfers knowledge from image aesthetic assessment using a pretrained IAA head to produce frame-wise aesthetic priors, employs peak-end rule-based temporal aggregation, an aesthetic rhythm encoder to model temporal progression, and a dynamic gated fusion mechanism for robustness. Built on a frozen ViT with few trainable parameters, it achieves state-of-the-art performance on VADB and cross-domain DIVIDE-3K benchmarks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Video aesthetic assessment (VAA) is far less explored than other visual assessment tasks due to scarcity of large-scale benchmarks and the intrinsic subjectivity of aesthetic judgment shaped by human perception. The authors aim to address these challenges by revisiting VAA from a psychological perspective, leveraging the peak-end rule to better align with how humans judge temporal experiences.

Method: The method introduces Peak-End-Net, which uses a frozen Vision Transformer (ViT) backbone and a pretrained IAA head to generate frame-wise aesthetic priors. These priors guide a peak-end rule-based temporal aggregation that focuses on salient moments and the ending. An aesthetic rhythm encoder captures temporal progression beyond isolated moments, and a dynamic gated fusion mechanism refines the assessment to improve robustness under distribution shift. The model is parameter-efficient with only a small number of trainable parameters.

Result: Extensive experiments on two VAA benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art performance: in-domain evaluation on VADB and cross-domain testing on DIVIDE-3K, affirming the effectiveness of psychologically grounded modeling for VAA.

Conclusion: The proposed psychologically grounded approach (Peak-End-Net) achieves state-of-the-art results on VAA benchmarks, validating that incorporating insights from human perception (peak-end rule) can significantly improve video aesthetic assessment while maintaining parameter efficiency and interpretability.

Abstract: Video aesthetic assessment (VAA) aims to predict how aesthetically pleasing a video is, yet remains far less explored than other visual assessment tasks. Its progress is hindered not only by the scarcity of large-scale benchmarks, but also by the intrinsic subjectivity of aesthetic judgment, which is shaped by human perception. In this paper, we revisit VAA from a psychological perspective and propose \textit{Peak-End-Net}, a lightweight and interpretable framework inspired by the \textit{peak-end rule}, which suggests that people tend to judge a temporal experience mainly according to its salient moments and the ending. Building on this intuition, we first transfer knowledge from image aesthetic assessment (IAA) to VAA by introducing a pretrained IAA head to produce frame-wise aesthetic priors, which serve as surrogate signals for identifying aesthetically salient moments and guiding \textit{peak-end rule}-based temporal aggregation. To further capture how a video evolves aesthetically over time, we design an aesthetic rhythm encoder that models temporal progression beyond isolated moments. Additionally, we refine the overall assessment through a dynamic gated fusion mechanism to improve robustness under distribution shift. Our method is built on a frozen vision transformer (ViT) and requires only a small number of trainable parameters, making it scalable and parameter-efficient. Extensive experiments on two existing VAA benchmarks, including in-domain evaluation on VADB and cross-domain testing on DIVIDE-3K, demonstrate that our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance, affirming the value of psychologically grounded modeling for VAA. Our code and models are available at https://github.com/AMAP-ML/Peak-End-Net.

</details>


### [78] [PlumeQuant: Uncertainty-aware consistency assessment of methane plume masks and emission-rate estimates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13945)
*Parisa Masnadi Khiabani,Wolfgang Jentner,Alireza Rangrazjeddi,Michael C. Wimberly,Binbin Weng,David Ebert,Charles Nicholson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过63个EMIT/Carbon Mapper羽流记录，证明已发布的标量参数（集成质量增强IME、羽流长度、排放速率）无法唯一约束羽流边界——不同但合理的掩膜可得到相同参数值。遗传算法（GA）集成使这种“等终性”显式化：高置信度核心掩膜仅覆盖中位数13%的可能足迹包络。开发了PlumeQuant工具用于重新计算这些参数并评估四种掩膜表示，其中CM-like掩膜无需参考数据即可高一致性复现已发布产品（IME中位数偏差+0.72%，排放速率+0.16%，IoU中位数0.843），但强调这些是产品级一致性诊断而非独立验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成像光谱仪生成的点源甲烷羽流产品中，掩膜、IME、羽流长度、排放速率及不确定性在物理和算法上相互关联。然而作者质疑这些已发布的标量量是否真正唯一地确定了羽流边界，即是否存在多种合理的羽流掩膜都能产生相同的产品参数值，这种“等终性”问题会影响产品解读和后续应用。

Method: 使用63个EMIT导出的Carbon Mapper羽流记录（来自27个场景）。提出PlumeQuant工具，可依据既定约定从分布式产品组件重新计算IME、羽流长度、排放速率及五项不确定性。开发了GA集成方法——以已发布的IME和羽流长度为条件，生成大量目标一致的掩膜，揭示可能足迹包络。构建CM-like掩膜（Carbon Mapper知情模拟），该掩膜在每次羽流生成时无参考掩膜或已发布量访问，参数在44羽流开发集（与场景不重叠）上一次性固定。

Result: 1) 已发布标量无法唯一约束羽流边界：GA集成的高置信度核心掩膜仅覆盖中位数13%的可能足迹包络，弱羽流和低重叠羽流模糊度最大。2) CM-like掩膜复现已发布IME的中位数偏差+0.72%，排放速率偏差+0.16%（平均绝对6.98%），中位数IoU=0.843，中位数不确定性尺度比=1.01。3) 留出集平均绝对误差：IME 7.6%，长度9.5%，排放速率6.1%。4) 这些被定性为产品级一致性诊断，而非独立验证，可标记弱、偏移或模糊羽流以供专家审查。

Conclusion: 已发布的甲烷羽流产品标量参数存在等终性问题——多种合理掩膜可产生相同参数集，尤其对于弱信号、低重叠羽流。PlumeQuant及GA集成方法可用于揭示这种不确定性，并生成更透明的产品一致性诊断。CM-like掩膜虽能高度复现已发布产品，但作者强调这仅是产品级内部一致性检查，不能替代独立的外部验证。建议将此类诊断用于专家审查流程，标识需要人工干预的歧义羽流。

Abstract: Imaging spectrometers increasingly distribute source-resolved methane plume products in which the plume mask, integrated mass enhancement (IME), plume length, emission rate, and uncertainty are physically and algorithmically linked. Using 63 EMIT-derived Carbon Mapper plume records from 27 scenes, we show that these published scalar quantities do not uniquely constrain the plume boundary: substantially different yet plausible masks reproduce the same IME, plume length, and emission rate. Genetic-algorithm (GA) ensembles conditioned on the published IME and plume length make this equifinality explicit: the high-confidence core selected by nearly all target-consistent masks covers a median of 13% of the plausible footprint envelope, and ambiguity is largest for weak, low-overlap plumes. The diagnostics come from PlumeQuant, which recomputes IME, plume length, emission rate, and five-term uncertainty from distributed product components under stated conventions and evaluates four mask representations: the distributed reference mask, a transparent Carbon Mapper-informed analogue (CM-like), the GA ensemble, and optional expert edits. The CM-like mask is generated per plume without access to the reference mask or published quantities, with settings fixed once on a scene-disjoint 44-plume development split. It reproduced published IME with +0.72% median difference and emission rate with +0.16% (6.98% mean absolute), reached 0.843 median intersection-over-union against the reference masks, and matched the published uncertainty scale (median ratio 1.01). Holdout mean absolute errors were 7.6% (IME), 9.5% (length), and 6.1% (rate). These are product-level consistency diagnostics, not independent validation. They flag weak, offset, or ambiguous plumes for expert review.

</details>


### [79] [CF-Net: Conflict Fusion with Speaker Normalisation and Certainty Weighting for Ambivalence/Hesitancy Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13976)
*Tung Hung Bui,Hong Hai Nguyen,Van Thong Huynh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CF-Net多模态网络，通过显式建模跨模态不一致性检测视频中的矛盾与犹豫情绪，在BAH数据集上达到Macro F1 0.7364。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频中的矛盾与犹豫情绪信号本身模糊，且通过细微的跨模态不一致性（而非典型情感）表达，现有方法难以有效检测。

Method: 使用冻结的SigLIP2（视觉）、HuBERT（音频）和DistilBERT（文本）提取特征，按说话人归一化减少身份泄露；通过ConflictFusion模块显式计算两两模态间的不一致性；采用确定性加权焦点损失、流形混合和模态丢弃训练；辅助确定性回归头利用模糊标注稳定学习。

Result: BAH验证集Macro F1为0.7155，私有挑战测试集Macro F1为0.7364（AP=0.7492）。

Conclusion: 显式建模跨模态矛盾是检测矛盾与犹豫情绪的有效策略，ConflictFusion及辅助确定性回归等设计有效提升了模糊样本下的识别性能。

Abstract: Detecting ambivalence and hesitancy (AH) in unconstrained video is challenging because the target signal is inherently ambiguous and expressed through subtle cross-modal incongruence rather than prototypical affect. We present CF-Net, a deep multimodal network submitted to the 3rd Edition of the AH Video Recognition Challenge (ABAW 11th, ECCV 2026), targeting the BAH dataset. CF-Net encodes visual, audio, and transcript streams with frozen SigLIP2, HuBERT, and DistilBERT backbones, normalises backbone features per speaker to reduce identity leakage, and fuses them via a ConflictFusion module that explicitly computes pairwise cross-modal incongruence. Training combines certainty-weighted focal loss, manifold mixup, and modality dropout; an auxiliary certainty-regression head leverages ambiguity annotations to stabilise learning on genuinely borderline samples. CF-Net achieves a Macro F1 of 0.7155 on the BAH validation set and 0.7364 (AP = 0.7492) on the private challenge test set.

</details>


### [80] [Music-to-Dance Generation via Atomic Movements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13978)
*Xinhao Cai,Yixuan Sun,Minghang Zheng,Qingchao Chen,Xin Jin,Song-chun Zhu,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种结构感知的音乐驱动舞蹈生成框架，将舞蹈建模为原子运动（可解释的运动事件）序列，通过两阶段生成（原子运动规划+过渡感知完成）实现结构连贯、节奏对齐且可控的舞蹈生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将运动建模为连续信号，忽略了舞蹈的组合性质，导致生成舞蹈结构不连贯且难以控制。

Method: 1) 对大规模舞蹈数据进行分割并聚类，构建原子运动词汇表；2) 使用大语言模型对聚类结果进行语义重新标注和细化；3) 两阶段生成框架：第一阶段预测原子运动的类型、时长和时序（符号化舞蹈分配），第二阶段由过渡感知生成器合成平滑且风格一致的运动。

Result: 与现有基线相比，所提方法在结构连贯性、节奏对齐和感知自然度方面显著提升，同时通过显式结构表示提供了更强的可解释性和可控编辑能力。

Conclusion: 将舞蹈分解为可解释的原子运动并采用两阶段规划-完成范式，能有效提升生成舞蹈的结构质量与控制性，验证了显式结构建模在舞蹈生成中的重要性。

Abstract: Music-driven dance generation aims to produce human motion that is both rhythmically synchronized and semantically consistent with music. While recent neural approaches have achieved impressive visual realism, they typically model motion as a continuous signal and neglect its compositional nature, making generated dances structurally incoherent and difficult to control. In this work, we introduce a structure-aware framework that models choreography as a sequence of atomic movements-semantically interpretable motion events that serve as the building blocks of dance. To construct this atomic movement vocabulary, we first segment large-scale dance data and cluster them into atomic movement groups. We then employ a large language model to semantically relabel and refine the clusters, yielding a set of interpretable and reusable atomic movements. Based on these atomic movement annotations, we design a two-stage generation framework that mirrors the human choreography process. In the atomic movement planning stage, the model predicts the type, duration, and timing of atomic movements conditioned on the input music, forming a symbolic dance allocation. In the completion stage, a transition-aware generator synthesizes smooth and stylistically coherent motion conditioned on the planned structure. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method produces dances with significantly improved structural coherence, rhythmic alignment, and perceptual naturalness compared to existing baselines, while providing enhanced interpretability and controllable editing through explicit structural representation.

</details>


### [81] [Screening Is Effective for Visual Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13983)
*Shunya Shimomura,Kazuhiro Hotta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VisionScreen，将语言建模中的Screening机制扩展到视觉识别，通过独立评估查询-键相似性并显式排除低相关性的图像块，替代传统ViT中基于softmax的相对注意力机制，在图像分类任务上超越ViT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ViT的自注意力机制使用softmax归一化的相对权重，难以独立评估补丁之间的相关性，且无法明确排除背景或冗余补丁，导致特征聚合中包含不必要的信息。

Method: VisionScreen将语言建模中的Screening机制扩展到二维空间域，将图像块视为排列在二维网格上的令牌，基于查询-键相似性独立评估每个补丁的相关性，通过阈值显式排除低相关性补丁，使每个补丁仅选择性聚合内容相关和空间相关的补丁，无需依赖补丁间的竞争。

Result: 在图像分类基准实验上，VisionScreen优于传统ViT。

Conclusion: Screening机制可以有效应用于视觉识别，为基于softmax注意力的相对特征聚合提供了一种替代方案。

Abstract: Vision Transformer (ViT) has been widely used as a powerful framework for modeling global dependencies among image patches. However, its core component, self-attention assigns softmax-normalized relative weights to all patches, making it difficult to evaluate the relevance between patches independently. In visual recognition, images often contain many background or redundant patches, yet self-attention cannot explicitly reject such irrelevant patches, which may introduce unnecessary information into feature aggregation. To address this limitation, Screening has been proposed in the field of language modeling, where the relevance of each token is independently evaluated based on query-key similarity and low-relevance tokens are explicitly excluded through thresholding. In this work, we propose VisionScreen, a new vision model that extends Screening mechanism to visual recognition. VisionScreen treats image patches as tokens arranged on a two-dimensional grid and extends absolute relevance estimation based on query-key similarity to the two-dimensional spatial domain. This allows each patch to selectively aggregate only content-wise and spatially relevant patches without relying on competition among patches. Experiments on image classification benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed method outperforms conventional ViT. These results suggest that Screening can be effective for visual recognition, offering an alternative to relative feature aggregation based on softmax attention.

</details>


### [82] [Task-Specific Feature Fusion Method for Multi-Task Affective Behavior Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13986)
*Jiajun Sun,Zhe Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出任务自适应特征融合策略用于ABAW11多任务情感行为分析，通过冻结两个预训练视觉骨干提取互补特征，并为不同任务（效价-唤醒度、分类表情、面部动作单元）选择特定的融合与预测策略，在验证集上取得1.6341的综合得分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管效价-唤醒度、分类表情和面部动作单元三个任务在面部行为上自然相关，但实验表明它们受益于不同的视觉特征、时序处理策略、融合机制和校准过程。因此需要为每个任务自适应地选择最优的融合与预测方式，而非强制所有任务共享单一架构。

Method: 1) 使用两个预训练视觉骨干（DINOv2 ViT-L和DINOv3 ConvNeXt-base）在外部表情数据集上微调，然后冻结提取互补帧级特征；2) 系统比较多种头部结构：帧级预测头、时序卷积头、时序平滑、LightGBM模型、特征拼接、门控融合、残差融合、晚期logit融合、阈值校准及共享MTL结构；3) 为每个任务选择特定的融合与预测策略。

Result: 在ABAW11验证集上：EXPR宏F1为0.4222，AU宏F1为0.5402，平均VA CCC为0.6717，综合验证分数为1.6341。

Conclusion: 任务自适应融合冻结视觉特征是一种简单有效的ABAW风格多任务情感行为分析策略。

Abstract: The 11th Affective Behavior Analysis in-the-wild (ABAW11) Multi-Task Learning Challenge requires a unified system to predict valence-arousal, categorical expressions, and facial action units from the official s-Aff-Wild2 images. Although these tasks are naturally related through facial behavior, our validation experiments show that they benefit from different visual features, temporal processing strategies, fusion mechanisms, and calibration procedures. In this paper, we study task-adaptive feature fusion for ABAW11 multi-task affective behavior analysis. We first adapt two pretrained visual backbones, DINOv2 ViT-L and DINOv3 ConvNeXt-base, on an external expression-oriented facial image set and then freeze them to extract complementary frame-level features from the official ABAW11 data. On top of these frozen features, we systematically compare frame-level prediction heads, temporal convolutional heads, post-hoc temporal smoothing, LightGBM models, feature concatenation, gated fusion, residual fusion, late logit fusion, threshold calibration, and shared MTL structures. The final system selects task-specific fusion and prediction strategies rather than forcing all tasks to share a single architecture. On the ABAW11 validation set, the selected system achieves an EXPR macro-F1 of 0.4222, an AU macro-F1 of 0.5402, and a mean VA CCC of 0.6717, resulting in an overall validation score of 1.6341. The results suggest that task-adaptive fusion of frozen visual features is a simple and effective strategy for ABAW-style multi-task affective behavior analysis.

</details>


### [83] [M$^\text{4}$World: A Multi-view Multimodal Driving World Model for Interactive Object Manipulation and Minute-long Streaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14005)
*Ke Cheng,Hanqiao Ye,Lei Shi,Yahui Liu,Yunhan Shen,Jingtao Dong,Zhenke Wang,Wenxuan Ao,Weixiang Xu,Kaining Huang,Shuhan Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: M⁴World是一个多视角多模态生成式驾驶世界模型，能够合成未来环视视频流与同步激光雷达扫描数据，支持交互式物体操控和稳定的分钟级长时生成，通过灵活的条件接口实现细粒度物体控制，并采用多阶段训练框架实现仅需四步去噪的在线因果生成，同时引入少样本后训练和视觉参考条件生成模型以支持长尾场景定制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶世界生成方法在物体级可控性和长时稳定性方面存在局限，无法同时满足细粒度物体操控与分钟级稳定生成的需求，亟需一种兼具高质量生成、精确可控性与长时间稳定性的驾驶仿真方案。

Method: 提出M⁴World模型，包含三方面技术：(1) 灵活条件接口，显式控制单个物体的空间布局与视觉外观；(2) 多阶段训练框架，仅需四步去噪即可实现在线因果生成，维持长序列中一致的世界动态；(3) 高效少样本后训练与视觉参考条件生成模型，保留通用生成能力的同时支持长尾场景定制。此外还引入了基于VLM的自动化评判流水线，评估场景级条件一致性、视角级物体可控性与跨视角物体一致性。

Result: 综合实验表明，M⁴World在生成质量、精确可控性和分钟级稳定流式生成方面均表现优异，并通过长尾增强和场景编辑等下游任务验证了其在可控、可扩展驾驶仿真中的潜力。

Conclusion: M⁴World通过多视角多模态生成、交互式物体操控与长时稳定生成三大核心能力，有效解决了现有方法在物体级可控性和长时稳定性上的不足，为可控、可扩展的驾驶仿真提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Driving-world generation has emerged as a core capability for scalable autonomous-driving simulation, yet existing methods remain limited in object-level controllability and long-horizon stability. We present M$^\text{4}$World, a Multi-view and Multimodal generative driving world model that synthesizes future surround-view video streams and synchronized LiDAR scans while supporting interactive object Manipulation and stable Minute-long streaming. Fine-grained object manipulation is realized through a flexible conditioning interface that supports explicit control over both the spatial layout and visual appearance of individual objects. Stable minute-long streaming, on the other hand, is achieved through a multi-stage training framework that enables online causal generation in only four denoising steps while maintaining coherent world dynamics throughout extended rollouts. Building on these components, we introduce an efficient few-clip post-training as well as a suite of visual reference-conditioned generation models, preserving general generation ability while allowing rare-case customization for long-tail controllability. To assess controllability beyond realism, we further introduce an automated VLM-based judging pipeline that evaluates scene-level condition adherence, view-wise object controllability, and cross-view object consistency. Comprehensive experiments show that M$^\text{4}$World consistently delivers high generation quality, precise controllability, and stable minute-long streaming. Together with downstream long-tail augmentation and scene editing, these results demonstrate the potential of M$^\text{4}$World for controllable, scalable driving simulation.

</details>


### [84] [Multi-Expert Routing for Multi-Domain Low-Resource OCR: A Manchu Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14041)
*Zhan Chen,Jiqiao Ma,Chih-wen Kuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对历史满文OCR中多种书写风格（楷书、行书、奏折半草书）识别问题，提出了一种多专家路由系统：利用迭代微调过程中的检查点作为领域专家，并通过轻量级页面级图像分类器按视觉风格分发页面。在三个冻结测试集上，路由系统达到了与各风格专家相当的高精度（楷书CER 0.30%，奏折1.57%，行书4.83%），路由器页面级领域准确率达99.3%，且两位专家并非针对其最终领域专门训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史满文OCR必须处理多种视觉上截然不同的书写风格（楷书、行书、奏折半草书），但标注数据有限，传统单一模型难以同时兼顾所有风格。

Method: 提出多专家路由系统：1）从迭代微调过程中复用检查点作为领域专家；2）训练轻量级页面级图像分类器作为路由器，按视觉风格将页面分派给最合适的专家；3）若检查点池中缺少合适专家，则为该领域额外训练一个专家。

Result: 在三个冻结测试集上，路由系统与各风格选定专家精度一致（楷书CER 0.30%，奏折1.57%，行书4.83%）。路由器页面级领域准确率达99.3%，与领域标签先知性能相同。三位专家中有两位并非针对其最终领域专门训练（仅行书专家是以目标领域训练的）。

Conclusion: 本文验证了多专家路由系统在历史满文OCR中的有效性，能以较少标注数据通过复用微调检查点实现多风格高精度识别，并为复现提供了完整的评估协议、路由器设计和每页预测细节。

Abstract: Historical Manchu OCR must accommodate various visually distinct writing styles, including regular script, running script, and the semi-cursive chancery hand used in palace memorials, despite limited labeled data. We study a multi-expert system that reuses checkpoints from an iterative fine-tuning process as domain specialists and uses a lightweight page-level image classifier to dispatch pages by visual style. When the checkpoint pool lacks a suitable specialist, we train an additional expert for that domain. On three frozen test sets, the routed system matches the selected specialist for each style at two-decimal precision: 0.30 percent CER on regular script, 1.57 percent on memorials, and 4.83 percent on running script. The router achieves 99.3 percent page-level domain accuracy and matches the domain-label oracle at the same precision. Two of the three selected specialists were not trained specifically for their final domain; only the running-script expert was trained with that domain as its target. We report the evaluation protocol, router design, and per-page predictions to make the comparison reproducible.

</details>


### [85] [From Pixels to States: Rethinking Interactive World Models as Game Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14076)
*Zhen Li,Zian Meng,Shuwei Shi,Mingliang Zhai,Jiaming Tan,Chuanhao Li,Kaipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文以传统游戏引擎的"动作-状态-观测"循环为组织视角，系统审视了基于视频生成模型构建交互式游戏世界的四个关键维度：玩家动作控制、游戏状态动态、状态-观测持久性以及实时交互生成，并介绍了《黑神话：悟空》超90小时帧对齐游戏数据的可扩展数据引擎。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建能对玩家动作做出连贯响应的交互式游戏世界是计算机图形学、游戏和人工智能的共同目标。尽管视频生成模型被视为潜在的新一代游戏引擎，但真正实现交互式游戏世界需要满足规则遵循、长程因果和实时生成等核心要求，现有研究缺乏系统性的审视框架。

Method: 本文以传统游戏引擎的"动作→状态更新→观测渲染"循环作为组织框架，从四个维度（玩家动作控制、游戏状态动态、状态-观测持久性、实时交互生成）分析现有方法，将各类方法归纳为代表性家族并讨论其优势与权衡。同时，为《黑神话：悟空》构建了包含玩家动作、真实游戏状态、视觉观测及结构化语义注释的超过90小时帧对齐游戏数据引擎。

Result: 提出了一个系统性的分析框架，覆盖交互式游戏世界建模的四个关键维度，并构建了一个大规模、高质量、状态感知的游戏数据集（>90小时，帧对齐的动作、状态与观测数据），为后续研究提供资源支撑。

Conclusion: 本文旨在提供一幅清晰的领域现状图景，推动基于视频生成模型的交互式游戏世界研究向前发展，尤其强调了状态感知建模和持久性因果推理的重要性。

Abstract: Building interactive worlds that respond coherently to player actions has long been a shared goal of computer graphics, games, and artificial intelligence. Recent video generative models provide a data-driven route toward this goal by predicting future observations conditioned on user actions, and are increasingly regarded as potential next-generation game engines. Realizing a genuinely interactive game world, however, requires interaction outcomes that follow rules over evolving game conditions, consequences that persist over long horizons, and a generation loop that operates in real time. Conventional game engines realize these properties through a recurrent action-state-observation loop, in which player actions update an explicit game state according to predefined rules and observations are rendered from the resulting state. Taking this loop as an organizing lens, this paper examines interactive game world modeling along four dimensions: player action control, game state dynamics, state-observation persistence, and real-time interactive generation. For each dimension, we start from the capabilities required by an interactive game world, group existing approaches into representative families, and discuss the strengths and trade-offs of each family. Complementing this analysis, we present a scalable data engine for Black Myth: Wukong that collects over 90 hours of gameplay with frame-aligned player actions, ground-truth game states, and visual observations, together with structured and semantic annotations, as a resource for state-aware game world modeling. We hope this paper offers a clear picture of where the field stands and fosters progress toward interactive game worlds.

</details>


### [86] [VideoRAE: Taming Video Foundation Models for Generative Modeling via Representation Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14088)
*Zhihao Xie,Junfeng Wu,Xinting Hu,Junchao Huang,Li Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VideoRAE，利用冻结的视频基础模型（如V-JEPA 2）的多尺度层次特征，通过轻量级1D自注意力投影器压缩成紧凑且适合生成的视频潜变量，支持连续潜变量（DiT）和离散token（自回归模型），在UCF-101上取得SOTA重建效果，且收敛速度比基线快约5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统3D-VAE主要针对像素级重建优化，限制了潜变量对语义和时空结构的捕捉能力；而视频基础模型虽具有强视频理解能力，但其冻结表示能否转化为紧凑、可重建且适合生成的视频潜变量尚未被探索。

Method: VideoRAE使用冻结的视频基础编码器提取多尺度层次特征，通过轻量级1D自注意力投影器进行压缩；支持连续潜变量（用于DiT）和通过多码书高维量化得到的离散token（用于自回归模型）；解码时采用局部-全局表示对齐目标与冻结的VFM教师进行语义保持训练，无需KL正则化。

Result: 在UCF-101上，VideoRAE在AR和DiT生成器下分别取得40和93的SOTA类到视频gFVD；收敛速度比竞争基线快约5倍；在2B规模文生视频实验中，替换LTX-VAE后实现更快的收敛。

Conclusion: 冻结的VFM表示可以作为通用且适合生成的视频潜变量，VideoRAE验证了这一设想的有效性。

Abstract: Video generative models commonly rely on latent spaces learned by 3D Variational Autoencoders (3D-VAEs). However, conventional 3D-VAEs are mainly optimized for pixel-level reconstruction, which can limit the semantic and spatio-temporal structure captured by their latents. Meanwhile, Video Foundation Models (VFMs) such as V-JEPA 2 and VideoMAEv2 show strong video understanding capabilities, yet whether their frozen representations can be transformed into compact, reconstruction-capable, and generation-friendly video latents remains largely unexplored. We answer this question with VideoRAE, a representation autoencoder that leverages multi-scale hierarchical features from a frozen video foundation encoder and compresses them with a lightweight 1D self-attention projector. VideoRAE supports both continuous latents for Diffusion Transformers and discrete tokens for autoregressive models via multi-codebook high-dimensional quantization. During decoding, a local-and-global representation alignment objective with the frozen VFM teacher improves semantic preservation and enables training without KL regularization. Experiments show that VideoRAE achieves strong reconstruction in both continuous and discrete regimes. On UCF-101, it obtains state-of-the-art class-to-video gFVDs of 40 and 93 with AR and DiT generators, respectively, while converging approximately 5x faster than competing autoencoder baselines. In a controlled 2B-scale text-to-video study, replacing LTX-VAE with VideoRAE leads to faster convergence under comparable settings. These results validate frozen VFM representations as versatile and generation-friendly video latents. The model and code will be released on https://zhxie0117.github.io/VideoRAE.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [87] [FixItFlow: Automated Troubleshooting Guide Generation from Cloud Incidents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13035)
*Srihari Unnikrishnan,Jaskaran Singh Walia,Drishti Goel,Supriyo Ghosh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 FixItFlow，一个利用大语言模型从历史事件数据自动生成故障排查指南的系统，通过提取诊断模式、合成结构化指南并严格验证防止虚构内容，在26名工程师评估中指南清晰度获61.5%正面评价，且将事件缓解时间降低2.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云服务故障频发，故障排查指南（Troubleshooting guides）对工程师快速诊断和恢复至关重要，但手动编写指南劳动密集，导致覆盖不全和文档过时。

Method: FixItFlow 系统：1) 从历史事件数据中提取工程师的诊断行为模式；2) 利用大语言模型合成结构化指南并附带已验证的命令；3) 执行严格验证以防止模型生成虚构/捏造内容。

Result: 在26名工程师的评估中，自动生成的指南在清晰度方面获得61.5%的正面评价；对于有相关指南的事件，缓解时间（mitigation time）降低了2.3倍。

Conclusion: 自动化指南生成能够在不增加文档负担的前提下提升事件响应效率，有效改善了工程师的故障排查体验。

Abstract: Cloud services experience frequent incidents that require rapid diagnosis and resolution. Troubleshooting guides help engineers respond consistently, but creating them manually is labor-intensive, resulting in incomplete coverage and outdated documentation. We present FixItFlow, an automated system that generates troubleshooting guides from historical incident data using large language models. The system extracts diagnostic patterns from engineer actions, synthesizes structured guides with verified commands, and enforces strict validation to prevent fabricated content. In our evaluation with 26 engineers, generated guides achieved 61.5\% positive ratings for clarity and demonstrated a 2.3x reduction in mitigation time for incidents with associated guides. These results indicate that automated guide generation can improve incident response while reducing documentation burden on engineering teams.

</details>


### [88] [Ask Before You Diagnose: Safe-Psych, a Sequential Evaluation Benchmark for LLMs in Psychiatry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13036)
*Oriana Presacan,Andreea Grama,Larisa Irimină,Alireza Nik,Jaya Ojha,Vajira Thambawita,Ciprian I. Băcilă,Bogdan Ionescu,Michael A. Riegler*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Safe-Psych是一个用于评估大语言模型在临床精神病学中处理逐步披露的、不完整信息能力的基准测试。它包含1000+真实精神病学临床笔记，分为三阶段标注（诊断/澄清/弃权）。评估发现，即使最强模型在面对不完整信息时也倾向于过早诊断（弃权不足超过60%），安全提示虽能减少草率诊断但导致过度弃权，且模型在序列评估中很少主动寻求澄清。Safe-Psych揭示了当前LLM在识别临床证据不完整方面存在根本性局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗基准假设模型可获得完整信息，但临床实践中证据常常是不完整或逐步披露的。当信息不足时，模型应请求澄清或弃权而非给出无支持的答案。需要专门的基准来评估LLM处理诊断不确定性的能力。

Method: 构建了Safe-Psych基准，含1000+真实精神病学临床笔记，将笔记分节以模拟逐步证据披露。每阶段由精神病学家标注三种动作标签：DIAGNOSE（诊断）、CLARIFY（澄清）、ABSTAIN（弃权）。在完整信息和序列设置下评估多个SOTA LLM。

Result: 大多数模型弃权不足超过60%；安全提示虽减少过早诊断但导致过度弃权；序列评估中模型常证据不足就诊断，很少主动澄清；过早诊断的准确性低于及时诊断。

Conclusion: Safe-Psych揭示了当前LLM在识别临床证据不完整和需要额外信息方面存在局限，即使能力强的模型也会在信息不足时做出不安全的决策。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for decision support in healthcare, but clinical evidence is often incomplete or evolving. When the available information is insufficient to support a reliable answer, models should request clarification or abstain rather than provide unsupported responses. Existing medical benchmarks, however, typically assume that complete information is available upfront. We introduce Safe-Psych, a sequential benchmark for evaluating how LLMs handle evolving diagnostic uncertainty in clinical psychiatry. Safe-Psych contains over 1,000 real-world psychiatric clinical notes segmented to simulate incremental evidence disclosure, with psychiatrist-derived action labels at each stage: DIAGNOSE, CLARIFY, or ABSTAIN. We evaluate multiple state-of-the-art LLMs in full-information and sequential settings. Our findings show that capability does not ensure calibration: even strong models struggle under incomplete clinical information, with under-abstention exceeding 60% for most models and safety-aware prompting reducing premature commitment only by shifting errors toward excessive abstention. In sequential evaluation, models frequently diagnose before sufficient evidence is available and rarely seek clarification unless explicitly prompted; these premature diagnoses are less accurate than on-time diagnoses. Overall, Safe-Psych reveals a limitation across the evaluated models: recognizing when clinical evidence is incomplete and additional information is needed. We release Safe-Psych to support research on improving LLM safety in healthcare.

</details>


### [89] [The Perplexity Trap: When Patent Law Makes Human Writing Look Like AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13044)
*Anubhab Banerjee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在消费级GPU（8GB显存）硬件约束下，现有开源零样本LLM文本检测器对欧洲专利权利要求书的检测均存在超过60%的假阳性率（Binoculars 78.3%, Fast-DetectGPT 61.3%, DetectGPT 80.5%），原因是EPC第84条要求清晰简洁，迫使人类撰写也进入低困惑度、低突发性空间，与LLM输出流形重叠。一个七特征语言学复杂度逻辑回归模型在同等硬件预算下达到74.0%准确率、28.1%假阳性率，比仅用困惑度的基线提升13个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EPO 2026年指南要求申请人对LLM辅助内容负责（第83条和细则42），但现有检测器在消费级GPU上对专利权利要求检测效果差，且人类撰写与LLM输出的语言特征空间重叠，需要开发适合该场景的检测方法。

Method: 在500件授权EPO H04电信专利与500件LLM生成对照（五种提示策略）上，评测三种零样本检测器（Binoculars, Fast-DetectGPT, DetectGPT）；通过Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct再生、LoRA适配Pythia-2.8B评分头、跨IPC复制（A61K, C07D, F03D）及H100复现验证结构性问题；提出七特征语言学复杂度逻辑回归模型。

Result: 在权利要求层面，Binoculars假阳性率78.3%，Fast-DetectGPT 61.3%，DetectGPT 80.5%；跨分类号平均假阳性率84.6%。七特征逻辑回归达到74.0%准确率、28.1%假阳性率，比困惑度基线提升13个百分点。

Conclusion: 当前零样本检测器无法可靠区分人类与LLM撰写的专利权利要求（结构性问题而非替代模型容量问题）；基于语言学复杂度的轻量级方法在消费级硬件上更有效，但仍有改进空间。

Abstract: The European Patent Office (EPO) reported record filings in 2025, and the 2026 EPO Guidelines hold applicants strictly responsible for LLM-assisted content under Article 83 and Rule 42, creating pressure to triage suspected AI-generated patent text. Two constraints make this hard. First, realistic prosecution settings often have only consumer GPUs with about 8 GB VRAM, not datacenter-class scoring stacks. Second, Article 84 of the European Patent Convention requires claims to be clear and concise, pushing human drafting onto the same low-perplexity, low-burstiness manifold that LLMs occupy. We benchmark three open-source zero-shot detectors on 500 granted EPO H04 telecom patents versus 500 LLM-generated counterparts using five prompting strategies, all under the consumer hardware envelope. At claim level, all detectors exceed 60 percent false-positive rate: Binoculars 78.3 percent, Fast-DetectGPT 61.3 percent, DetectGPT 80.5 percent. The failure persists under Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct regeneration, LoRA-adapted Pythia-2.8B scoring heads, cross-IPC replication on A61K, C07D, and F03D (mean FPR 84.6 percent), and H100 re-evaluation with published Falcon-7B and GPT-J-6B heads, arguing the issue is structural rather than substitute-model capacity. A seven-feature linguistic-complexity logistic regression reaches 74.0 percent accuracy at 28.1 percent FPR, a 13 percentage-point gain over a perplexity-only baseline at a comparable operating point, without using likelihood at inference and within the same hardware budget.

</details>


### [90] [Do LLMs Need Architectural Changes for Simultaneous Speech Translation? A Prefix-to-Prefix Data Driven Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13158)
*Junkun Chen,Jian Xue,Ming Tang,Abdel Heba,Hoda Gholami,Ruchao Fan,Jinyu Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CSSEL-P2P，一种用于同步语音翻译（SimulST）的简单数据驱动方法，采用固定长度分块、回滚式提交前缀和教师标注的前缀到前缀（P2P）目标，无需架构更改即可实现高效流式翻译。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解码器-only LLM在SimulST中面临上下文有限和跨语序重排的挑战，现有方法需要架构改动或显式读写策略，在处理会话语音时因分割边界模糊而脆弱。

Method: 使用固定长度分块进行累积流式解码，结合回滚式提交前缀；采用教师标注的前缀到前缀（P2P）目标并引入有界等待机制进行微调，形成CSSEL-P2P。

Result: 在内部会话语音评估中，CSSEL-P2P在相近延迟（+0.15s平均滞后）下，流式翻译质量比CSSEL基线提升+1.54 COMETKiwi。

Conclusion: 通过P2P监督，无需架构更改即可实现有效的SimulST。

Abstract: Simultaneous speech translation (SimulST) requires incremental translation under strict latency constraints, yet remains challenging for decoder-only LLM systems due to limited context and cross-lingual reordering. Recent approaches often introduce architectural changes or explicit read/write policies to control output timing, which can be brittle in conversational speech where segmentation boundaries are ambiguous. We present a simple data-driven alternative: fixed-length chunks for cumulative streaming decoding with a rewind-based committed prefix, and teacher-labeled prefix-to-prefix (P2P) targets with bounded waiting for fine-tuning, yielding CSSEL-P2P, where CSSEL is our proposed chunked streaming speech encoder LLM. In our in-house conversational speech evaluation, CSSEL-P2P improves streaming quality by +1.54 COMETKiwi over the CSSEL streaming baseline at comparable latency (+0.15s Average Lagging), suggesting effective SimulST without architectural changes via P2P supervision.

</details>


### [91] [What Models Express, Suppress, and Resist: Auditing Open-Weight LLMs with Persona Vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13162)
*Winston Zeng,Ali Emami,Jinho Choi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地在大规模上应用人格向量（persona vectors）方法，构建了一个包含53种特质的清单，覆盖四个行为领域，并在两个开放权重模型中标记每种特质为自然（基线表达）、可引导但可放大、或难以提取。研究表明，模型默认表现为有帮助、任务导向的行为；所有代理特质均为自然，临床医生特质与独立认证心理学家的偏好判断在17项中匹配16项。引导操作在默认排除的特质（如夸张、幻觉、谄媚）上产生最大增益。特质间的非对称性普遍存在：两个可引导特质可以组合，但涉及默认特质的配对则不行。当标准提取失败时，从微调变体转移的向量仍能恢复特质，而残差拒绝出现在模型思维链中。人格向量最有价值不是作为控制手段，而是作为行为组织的探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究仅覆盖少量特质，缺乏对语言模型行为组织（什么行为会自然表达、隐藏或抵抗）的系统性大规模探测。人格向量虽能揭示行为方向，但尚未在足够规模和多样性上进行应用。本文旨在填补这一空白，通过大规模特质清单系统性地评估模型的行为组织。

Method: 作者构建了包含53种特质的清单，覆盖四个行为上不同的领域（代理特质、临床医生特质、通用特质等）。使用人格向量方法，在两个开放权重模型中标记每种特质为自然（基线可表达）、可引导但可放大（潜在但可通过引导放大）、或难以提取（对标准提取抵抗）。通过向量转移和思维链分析探究失败案例。

Result: 两个模型默认表现为有帮助、任务导向行为；所有九种代理特质均为自然；临床医生特质中16/17与认证心理学家的独立偏好判断匹配。引导操作在默认排除的特质（夸张、幻觉、谄媚）上产生最大收益。171个通用特质配对中均表现出非对称性：两个可引导特质可组合，但涉及默认特质的配对不会。当标准提取对“邪恶”等特质失败时，从微调变体转移的向量仍能恢复，且残余拒绝出现在模型的思维链中。

Conclusion: 人格向量最有价值的用途不是作为一组控制手段，而是作为探测行为组织的探针。该方法揭示了语言模型内部行为组织的结构，包括自然表达、可引导但隐藏、以及难以提取的特质之间的非对称关系。

Abstract: What a language model will and will not do is largely set during post-training, but which behaviors it expresses, hides, or resists is not revealed by prompting alone. Persona vectors, behavioral directions in activation space, can probe this organization, but prior work covers only a handful of traits. We present the first systematic application of persona vectors at this scale, compiling a 53-trait inventory across four behaviorally distinct domains and labeling every trait in two open-weight models as natural (expressed at baseline), steerable latent but amplifiable, or intractable (resistant to standard extraction). Both models default to helpful, task-oriented behavior: all nine agentic traits are natural, and their default clinician behavior matches a board-certified psychologist's independent desirability judgments on 16 of 17 traits. Steering produces its largest gains on traits these defaults exclude: hyperbole, hallucination, and sycophancy. The same asymmetry holds across all 171 generic-trait pairs: two steerable traits can collapse the composition, but pairs involving a default never do. Where standard extraction fails on a trait like "evil," a vector transferred from a fine-tuned variant still recovers it, with the residual refusals appearing inside the model's chain-of-thought. Persona vectors are most informative not as a set of controls but as a probe of behavioral organization.

</details>


### [92] [Text2Sign: A Single-GPU Diffusion Baseline for Text-to-Sign Language Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13164)
*Ruize Xia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Text2Sign presents a text-conditioned diffusion model for generating short sign-language video clips on a single NVIDIA L4 GPU. It uses a frozen vision-language text encoder with a 3D encoder-decoder and factorized spatiotemporal attention to reduce cost. The model achieves limited results (SSIM 0.24, PSNR 15.11 dB) on low-resolution 64×64 clips, and serves as a research baseline rather than a production system.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sign language is a primary communication channel for Deaf and hard-of-hearing people, but text-to-signer video generation is costly because video diffusion models are expensive to train and evaluate. The authors aim to reduce this cost to enable single-GPU operation.

Method: Text2Sign combines a frozen vision-language text encoder (e.g., CLIP) with a 3D encoder-decoder and factorized spatiotemporal attention. The paper compares convolution-only vs transformer backbones, frozen pretrained vs task-specific text encoders, and factorized vs full attention. It uses 8-step DDIM sampling with a guidance scale of 5.0.

Result: On a signer-disjoint How2Sign split: best short-run ablation validation loss 0.0648, longer-run checkpoint 0.00999. On a compact evaluation slice: SSIM 0.2403±0.0238, PSNR 15.11±0.42 dB, temporal consistency 1.0000±0.0000. Generates 32-frame 64×64 clip in 12.60 s (2.54 fps) with peak inference memory 3.12 GB. Denoising audit shows weak prompt sensitivity: removing text increases late-timestep loss from 0.9875 to 0.9891; shuffled prompts perform similarly to correct prompts.

Conclusion: The system is restricted to low-resolution, short clips and lacks expert linguistic evaluation. It should be viewed as a single-GPU research baseline rather than a complete sign-language production system. Code is available at https://github.com/xiaruize0911/text2sign.

Abstract: Sign language is a primary communication channel for millions of Deaf and hard-of-hearing people, yet text-to-signer video generation remains costly because video diffusion models are expensive to train and evaluate. This paper presents Text2Sign, a text-conditioned diffusion model for short sign-language clips that runs on a single NVIDIA L4 GPU. It combines a frozen vision-language text encoder with a 3D encoder-decoder and factorized spatiotemporal attention to reduce the cost of full-video attention while preserving motion coherence.
  We compare convolution-only and transformer-style backbones, frozen pretrained and task-specific text encoders, and factorized versus full attention. On a signer-disjoint How2Sign split, the best short-run ablation reaches a validation loss of 0.0648, while a longer-run checkpoint reaches 0.00999. On a compact evaluation slice, the latter achieves an SSIM of $0.2403 \pm 0.0238$, a PSNR of $15.11 \pm 0.42$ dB, and temporal consistency of $1.0000 \pm 0.0000$ using 8-step DDIM sampling with a guidance scale of 5.0. It generates a 32-frame, $64 \times 64$ clip in 12.60 seconds, or 2.54 frames per second, with peak inference memory of 3.12 GB.
  A held-out denoising audit shows only weak prompt sensitivity: removing text increases late-timestep loss from 0.9875 to 0.9891, while shuffled prompts perform similarly to correct prompts. Frozen text conditioning therefore improves short-budget validation loss, but prompt-specific separation remains limited. The system is restricted to low-resolution, short clips and lacks expert linguistic evaluation, so it should be viewed as a single-GPU research baseline rather than a complete sign-language production system. Code is available at https://github.com/xiaruize0911/text2sign.

</details>


### [93] [RAGthoven at SemEval-2026 Task 1: A Multi-Stage Pipeline Walks Into a Benchmark and Barely Clears the Bar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13189)
*Marek Šuppa,Viktória Ondrejová,Lucia Ganajová,Gregor Karetka,Daniel Skala*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RAGthoven 是一个用于 SemEval-2026 MWAHAHA 多语言幽默生成任务的多阶段 LLM 流水线系统，结合 RAG 和计算幽默理论，在英语、西班牙语和中文上与 Gemini 2.5 Flash 基线并列第一，但实验表明 agentic 变体并未带来显著提升，提示强基础模型下复杂 prompt 工程和 agent 脚手架的边际收益递减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在强基础大模型条件下，多阶段 prompt 工程和 agentic 脚手架对多语言幽默生成任务的边际收益，验证计算幽默理论（良性违规理论、脚本语义幽默理论）在 LLM 流水线中的可操作性。

Method: 构建多阶段 LLM 流水线（Planner → Best-of-N Writer → Reflector 自批判 → LLM-as-a-Judge 评判器），Planner 阶段引入 RAG 从 curated 笑话语料库检索增强；实验两种 agentic 变体（ReAct 风格顺序工具调用 Exp09 与自主多分支编排 Exp10），加入确定性 ConstraintAudit 检查器；在四个前沿模型上对比评估。

Result: 在英语、西班牙语、中文三种语言上与 Gemini 2.5 Flash 基线共享 Rank 1（统计置信区间重叠）；西班牙语中 RAGthoven Elo 领先基线 42 分（1182 vs 1140），英语和中文基线略高但统计无显著差异；两种 agentic 变体在 12 实例英语样本上未产生优于非 agentic 流水线的输出，但工具调用开销显著更高。

Conclusion: 在强基础模型（如 Gemini 2.5 Flash）参与的条件下，复杂的多阶段 prompt 工程和 agentic 脚手架带来的提升呈现语言依赖的递减趋势，简单基线即可达到同等水平。

Abstract: We present RAGthoven, our system for SemEval-2026 Task 1 (MWAHAHA), Subtask A (multilingual constrained humor generation in English, Spanish, and Chinese). RAGthoven decomposes creative text generation into a multi-stage large language model (LLM) pipeline (Planner, Best-of-N Writer, Reflector for self-critique, LLM-as-a-judge Judge) grounded in computational humor theory (Benign Violation Theory, Script-based Semantic Theory of Humor) and refined across ten experiments.
  In our final configuration, we augment the Planner with retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) from a curated joke corpus, seeding generation with diverse joke mechanisms. We also evaluate two agentic variants -- ReAct-style sequential tool-calling (Exp09) and autonomous multi-branch orchestration (Exp10) -- that expose the same four stages with a deterministic ConstraintAudit checker. Across four frontier models on a held-out 12-instance English sample, neither agentic variant produced outputs we judged superior to the non-agentic pipeline despite substantially higher tool-call budgets.
  RAGthoven shares Rank 1 with the Gemini 2.5 Flash baseline in all three languages, with overlapping organizer-reported confidence intervals. In Spanish, it leads the baseline by 42 raw Elo points (1182 vs. 1140), while in English (1045 vs. 1081) and Chinese (1045 vs. 1053) the baseline holds the higher raw rating within the same statistical tie.
  Together, these results suggest language-dependent diminishing returns from elaborate multi-stage prompt engineering and agentic scaffolding once a strong frontier model is in the loop.

</details>


### [94] [Adaptive Filtering of the KV Cache: Diagnosing and Correcting Structural-Role Bias in LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13205)
*Soumil Mandal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 基于注意力累积质量的KV缓存淘汰策略（如H2O）在处理结构化输入（如嵌套JSON）时，会不公平地保留结构性噪声（分隔符、空白、KEY标记）而非实质内容（VALUE标记），导致准确率从88%骤降至0%。本文提出一种无需重新训练、基于角色条件分配的方法（在SnapKV窗口得分上增加角色调节），通过抑制KEY标记，在低预算下缩小63-98%的H2O差距，高预算下甚至超越全缓存基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有注意力驱动的KV缓存淘汰方法（H2O及其变体）在结构化密集输入上表现不佳，因为累积注意力质量分数会将结构性标记（如JSON中的KEY、分隔符）误判为高重要性，导致信号-噪声比严重退化，准确率从88%崩溃至0%。

Method: 1. 分析了结构化输入下注意力累积分数的非平稳滤波特性，发现非内容角色（分隔符、空白）的能量比内容角色高一个数量级，KEY标记的保留率是VALUE的1.8倍。2. 通过反事实实验验证抑制KEY标记是最优可部署策略。3. 提出无需重新训练的角色条件分配方法，在SnapKV的窗口得分上施加角色条件调节（一个超参数），通过15MB线性角色探针以极低成本提供角色标签。

Result: 在低于20%的缓存预算下，该方法收窄了63-98%的H2O差距；在更高预算下，适度匹配或超越全缓存准确率（存在轻微的种子敏感的降噪效应，在B=0.50时边缘显著，B=0.30时与零不可区分）。精确匹配精度可从0%恢复至接近原始水平。

Conclusion: 角色条件化的KV缓存淘汰策略能有效缓解结构化输入下注意力驱动的缓存压缩导致的信号-噪声比退化问题。一个15MB的线性角色探针即可提供所需标签，但达到解析器级别的下游准确率仍有待解决。

Abstract: Attention-based KV cache eviction (H2O and its descendants) compresses the memory-constrained state of a long-context model by ranking tokens on accumulated attention mass, treated here as signal energy, and keeping the heaviest. On schema-dense input streams such as nested JSON, this score acts as a non-stationary filter that disproportionately retains noise: a non-content sink role (delimiters or whitespace) carries an order of magnitude more energy than any content role, and structural KEY tokens are over-retained at roughly 1.8x the rate of the answer-carrying VALUE tokens, collapsing exact-match accuracy from 88% to 0% at a 5% budget as the signal-to-noise ratio of the retained state degrades. A counterfactual experiment establishes that suppressing KEY tokens is the best deployable filter. Our retraining-free, role-conditional allocation over SnapKV's windowed score, governed by a single tuned hyperparameter, closes 63-98% of the H2O gap at sub-20% budgets and, at higher budgets, modestly matches or exceeds full-cache accuracy -- a small, seed-sensitive denoising effect (borderline significant at B=0.50; not distinguishable from zero at B=0.30 over four seeds). A 15 MB linear role probe supplies these labels at negligible inference cost, though matching parser-level downstream accuracy remains open.

</details>


### [95] [GSM-Plus-BN: A Perturbation-Based Benchmark for Bangla Mathematical Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13248)
*Bidyarthi Paul,Nahida Jannat Mayouree,Md. Asif Karim,Sagar Chandra Nath,Swastika Kundu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了GSM-Plus-BN，一个基于英文GSM-Plus基准的孟加拉语数学推理扰动数据集，并评估了六个开源LLM在孟加拉语数学推理上的表现，发现GPT-OSS-20B在标准提示下种子问题准确率最高达96.08%，而更大的模型如Llama-3.3-70B和GPT-OSS-120B在扰动类型上表现出更强的鲁棒性，CoT提示显著提升了推理能力，但所有模型与英文基准相比仍有显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数学推理评估主要针对英语等资源丰富的语言，忽视了孟加拉语等语言地区，导致AI发展不公平。孟加拉语有超过2.3亿使用者，但缺乏系统的孟加拉语数学推理基准，特别是扰动数据集，无法评估模型的鲁棒性和真正理解能力。

Method: 从英文GSM-Plus基准出发，通过人工翻译构建孟加拉语扰动数据集GSM-Plus-BN（包含1000个种子问题和8000个扰动变体）。在标准提示和思维链（CoT）提示下评估六个开源LLM（Qwen3-32B、Llama-3.1-8B-Instant、Llama-3.3-70B-Versatile、Llama-4-Scout-17B-16E-Instruct、GPT-OSS-120B、GPT-OSS-20B），共9000个评估样本。

Result: GPT-OSS-20B在标准提示下种子问题准确率最高（96.08%）；较大模型（Llama-3.3-70B、GPT-OSS-120B）对扰动类型的鲁棒性更强；CoT提示相比标准提示显著提升了多数模型的推理能力；但所有模型在孟加拉语上的表现均低于英文基准，表明扰动孟加拉语文本的固有困难。

Conclusion: 本研究通过提供GSM-Plus-BN数据集和基线结果，为未来孟加拉语数学推理研究奠定了重要基础，揭示了当前LLM在低资源语言数学推理上的不足，呼吁更多关注语言多样性。

Abstract: The evaluation of mathematical reasoning in large language models (LLMs) has predominantly focused on high-resource languages like English. This has created a significant barrier to the equitable development and deployment of AI in linguistically diverse regions such as Bangladesh, where over 230 million people speak Bengali. Despite this global significance, there has been minimal prior work on mathematical reasoning in Bengali and no existing research that systematically benchmarks a perturbated Bengali mathematical dataset, leaving a critical void in assessing model robustness and true comprehension beyond pattern recognition. This study addresses this gap by introducing GSM-Plus-BN, a novel perturbated Bengali mathematical dataset derived from the English GSM-Plus benchmark and verified by human translators. We evaluate six open-source LLMs Qwen3-32B, Llama-3.1-8B-Instant, Llama-3.3-70B-Versatile, Llama-4-Scout-17B-16E-Instruct, GPT-OSS-120B, and GPT-OSS-20B using a benchmark of 9,000 evaluation samples comprising 1,000 seed questions and 8,000 perturbed variants under both Standard Prompting and Chain-of-Thought (CoT) Prompting. Experimental results show that GPT-OSS-20B achieves the highest seed question accuracy of 96.08% under Standard Prompting, while larger models such as Llama-3.3-70B and GPT-OSS-120B demonstrate superior robustness across perturbation types. Furthermore, CoT prompting substantially improves reasoning for most models compared to Standard Prompting, yet a notable performance gap persists across all models relative to their English benchmarks, underscoring the inherent difficulty of perturbed Bengali text. This research makes a foundational contribution by providing GSM-PLUS-BN as a new resource and baseline for future Bengali mathematical reasoning research.

</details>


### [96] [Discourse-Aware Policy Analysis with Argumentation: A Hybrid LLM-Symbolic Framework for Disaster Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13260)
*Stylianos Loukas Vasileiou,Olga Derendiaeva*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Apaf，一种混合大语言模型与符号推理的流水线，将批判性话语分析操作化为量化双极论证框架。通过对政策文本中的论证进行协商框架与管理框架分类，并基于确定性规则识别四种框架中介关系（能动性削弱、议程转移、工具性支持、规范性支持），生成可解释的论证图。作者发布了包含美国、英国、加拿大、澳大利亚四国100份减灾政策子文档的数据集，并证明生成的论证图在多个司法管辖区中具有准确性、可解释性和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 政策文件的推理往往隐含不显，参与性承诺与管理性控制常共存于同一文本中，其张力很少被直接表述。现有计算方法无法表达驱动这些张力的框架中介关系（即一个论证通过窄化或工具化另一个论证而非直接反驳它），而大语言模型的端到端摘要虽流畅但缺乏领域专家可审查的结构。因此需要一种能显式建模政策论证中框架间张力的可解释方法。

Method: Apaf是一个混合LLM-符号流水线。首先将论证分类为协商框架或管理框架；然后通过基于LLM提取特征的确定性规则生成四种框架中介关系子类型：能动性削弱、议程转移、工具性支持和规范性支持。最终构建一个量化双极论证框架（bipolar argumentation framework），将政策文本中的论证关系形式化。

Result: 发布了包含100份来自美、英、加、澳四国减灾政策子文档的新数据集。实验表明，生成的论证图在准确性、可解释性和跨司法管辖区的稳定性方面表现良好。

Conclusion: Apaf通过混合方法成功将批判性话语分析量化为可计算的双极论证框架，能够显式揭示政策文本中不同框架间的张力关系，且在不同国家的政策文档上具有一致性和可靠性，为政策分析提供了可审查的结构化工具。

Abstract: Policy documents shape governance outcomes, but their reasoning is often implicit. Participatory commitments and managerial control routinely coexist in the same text, and the tensions between them are rarely stated directly. Existing computational approaches to policy discourse cannot express the frame-mediated relations that drive these tensions, where one argument narrows or instrumentalizes another rather than rejecting it. End-to-end summarization by large language models produces fluent text but offers little structure that domain experts can inspect or contest. We present Apaf, a hybrid LLM--symbolic pipeline that operationalizes critical discourse analysis as a quantitative bipolar argumentation framework over policy text. Arguments are first classified into deliberative or managerial frames. Four frame-mediated relation subtypes (agency reduction, agenda shift, instrumental support, and normative support) are then produced by deterministic rules over LLM-extracted features. We release a novel dataset of 100 sub-documents of disaster-risk-reduction policy from the USA, UK, Canada, and Australia, and show that the resulting argument graphs are accurate, interpretable, and stable across jurisdictions.

</details>


### [97] [Finding the Right Tables and Columns: A Benchmark and Corpus-Adaptive Embeddings for SQL Schema Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13311)
*Qingcheng Zeng,Puxuan Yu,Aman Mehta,Fuheng Zhao,Rajhans Samdani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将文本到SQL中的模式链接（schema linking）视为独立的检索任务，并针对大规模数据库场景提出了一种轻量级、无标注的语料自适应微调方法：通过从目标模式语料中直接合成自然语言查询、挖掘粒度感知的难负样本，对305M参数的嵌入模型进行对比学习微调，在多项基准上将召回率从60.4提升至75.6，使该小模型超越4-8B参数的大模型，并证明该增益来自可迁移的模式检索能力而非记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在文本到SQL任务中，当数据库包含数千张表/列时，模式检索（找出问题所需的表和列）成为一个先决且关键的问题，但现有工作缺乏对这一步的专门评估，且现成的文本/代码嵌入器在此场景下迁移效果差。

Method: 提出语料自适应微调（corpus-adaptive fine-tuning）：直接从目标模式语料合成自然语言查询（自监督），挖掘粒度感知的难负样本（区分表级与列级），对305M参数的嵌入器进行对比微调。同时将该方法应用于8B参数的大型嵌入器作为验证。
